<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 297]*

chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati . . . pe . . . chandapadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti viriyasamādhi-cittasamādhi-vīmaṃsāsamādhi-padhāna-saṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti . . . pe . . . saddhindriyaṃ bhāveti viriyindriyaṃ bhāveti satindriyaṃ bhāveti samādhindriyaṃ bhāveti paññindriyaṃ bhāveti . . . pe . . . saddhābalaṃ bhāveti viriyabalaṃ bhāveti samādhibalaṃ bhāveti paññābalaṃ bhāveti satisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti viriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti passaddhisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti upekhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāveti sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāveti sammāvācaṃ bhāveti sammākammantaṃ bhāveti sammā-ājīvaṃ bhāveti sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāveti sammāsatiṃ bhāveti sammāsamādhiṃ bhāveti. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave majjhimā paṭipadā. Imā kho bhikkhave tisso paṭipadā ti[^297-1].

153. Tīhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi tīhi? Attanā ca pāṇātipātī hoti parañ ca pāṇātipāte samādapeti pāṇātipāte ca samanuñño hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave tīhi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Tīhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi tīhi? Attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti parañ ca pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā samādapeti pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

154. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca adinnādāyī hoti parañ ca adinnādāne samādapeti adinnādāne ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca adinnādānā paṭivirato parañ ca . . . pe . . . adinnādānā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

155. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca kāmesu micchācārī hoti parañ ca


<!-- PTS Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 298]*

kāmesu micchācāre samādapeti kāmesu micchācāre ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti parañ ca kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā samādapeti kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

156. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca musāvādī hoti parañ ca musāvāde samādapeti musāvāde ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca musāvādā paṭivirato hoti parañ ca musāvādā veramaṇiyā samādapeti musāvādā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

157. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca pisuṇāvāco hoti parañ ca pisuṇāya vācāya samādapeti pisuṇāya vācāya ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca pisuṇāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti parañ ca pisuṇāya vācāya veramaṇiyā samādapeti pisuṇāya vācāya veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

158. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca pharusāvāco hoti parañ ca pharusāya vācāya samādapeti pharusāya vācāya ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti parañ ca pharusāya vācāya veramaṇiyā samādapeti pharusāya vācāya veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

159. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca samphappalāpī hoti parañ ca samphappalāpe samādapeti samphappalāpe ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti parañ ca samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā samādapeti samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . .

160. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca abhijjhālu hoti parañ ca abhijjhāya samādapeti abhijjhāya ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca anabhijjhālu hoti parañ ca anabhijjhāya samādapeti anabhijjhāya ca samanuñño hoti . . .


<!-- PTS Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 299]*

161. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca vyāpannacitto hoti parañ ca vyāpāde samādapeti vyāpāde ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca avyāpannacitto hoti parañ ca avyāpāde samādapeti avyāpāde ca samanuñño hoti . . .

162. . . . pe . . . Attanā ca micchādiṭṭhiko hoti parañ ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapeti micchādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñño hoti . . . pe . . . Attanā ca sammādiṭṭhiko hoti parañ ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapeti sammādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñño hoti. Imehi kho tīhi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti[^299-1].

163. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo? Suññato samādhi, animitto samādhi, appaṇihito samādhi. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā[^299-2]. Rāgassa bhikkhave pariññāya parikkhayāya pahānāya khayāya vayāya virāgāya nirodhāya cāgāya paṭinissaggāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Dosassa . . . pe . . . mohassa kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa abhiññāya pariññāya parikkhayāya pahānāya khayāya vayāya virāgāya nirodhāya cāgāya paṭinissaggāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^299-3]. Idam avoca Bhagavā. Attamanā te bhikkhū Bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinandun ti. Ekanipāti ca Dukanipāti ca Tikanipāti ca samatti[^299-4].


<!-- PTS Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 300]*

Aṅguttaranikāyavare sabbaññutaparamavisuddhadassanā nipātā ekādasa[^300-1] yeva pavattitā uddānato te nisāmetha ādito: I. Itthirūpaṃ purisarūpaṃ pañca nīvaraṇāni ca akammaniyādikaṃ pañca pañca cittaṃ anatthato (i.-iv.). Sūkaṃ paduṭṭharahado candano[^300-2] lahu pabhassaro āsave[^300-3] bhavamanasā bhāgīhi apare duve (v.-vi.6). Uppajjanti parihāni anatthāya asammosā[^300-4] catukoṭikā[^300-5] mukha[^300-6] ete caturo sabbavatthitā (vi.7-x.32). Adhammā vinayo ca bhāsitāciṇṇaṃ paññatti pañcamaṃ āpatti lahu duṭṭhulyaṃ sāvasesarakamena[^300-7] ca (x. 33 - xii.). Puggalo Sāriputto ca etadagge tath'; eva ca aṭṭhānañ ca nibbidādasampadā[^300-8] (xiii. - xvi.). Anuppaññañ ca kusalaṃ micchādiṭṭhi pavaḍḍhati yen'; eva sattā asaddhammavuṭṭhānena (xvii.- xviii. 2). Pare[^300-9] sāvajjakhipaṃ durakhāte[^300-10] ca nadvassaṃ[^300-11] manussesu majjhimā viññātariyena cakkhunā (xvii.3 - xviii.16). Dassanā savanā dhāraṇā upaparikkhaṇā attham aññāya dassaṃ[^300-12] saggo saṃviggena vassaggārammaṇena ca. Annena ca ye vuttā ye attharasena ca dve manussā dve devā nirayena[^300-13] apare duve. Dve tiracchānayoniyo dve pettivisayā Jambudīpesu yojaye (xix.). Araññe[^300-14] piṇḍapātaṃ paṃsukūladhammakathikā vinayena ca bahusaccathāvareyya-ākappā dve ca honti (xx.1). Parivārajjhānamettā upaṭṭhānaṃ[^300-15] padhāna-indriyabalabojjhaṅgamaggo abhibhāyatanavimokkhakasiṇena ca (xx. 2 - xx. 63).


<!-- PTS Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 301]*

Dve saññā anussate jhānā[^301-1] sahagatehi yojaye accharā ca mahā[^301-2]-samuddo saṃvegā passaddhi akusalaṃ kusalena ca (xx.63-xxi.16). Avijjā paññā pabhedo ca paṭivedho paṭisambhidā caturo phalena[^301-3] paṭilābho vuḍḍhi vepullatāya ca (xxi.17-xxi.31-4). Mahāputhuvepullañ ca gambhīraṃ asamantabhūriñ ca bāhu-sī<gha>-lahu-hāsu-java-tikkha-nibbedhena ca (xxi. 31-5 - xxi. 31-16). Bhuñjanti bhattā parihīnaṃ viraddhaṃ pamadiṃsu te muṭṭhāsevanabhāvanabahulā abhiññā-pariññāya ca atho sacchikiriyāya (xxi.17-70). Paṭhamo nipāto. II. Vajjappadhānatapanīyā atho pi uppaññāsiṃ saññojanañ ca kaṇhañ ca sukkam[^301-4] athavassam upagacche balabojjhaṅgajhānena desanā-vikaraṇena ca adhamma-cariyā akatattā ekaṃsaṃ akusalaṃ atho pi sammosā (i.-ii.). Bālo ca duṭṭho bhāsitañ ca neyatthā paṭichanna-diṭṭhisīlena vijjabhāgiyena ca (iii.). Bhūmi duppaṭikāro[^301-5] kiṃvādi[^301-6] dakkhiṇeyyā saññojanasamacittā caraṇakacoro[^301-7] paṭipatti vyañjanena ca (iv.). Uttānavaggā aggavatī ariyakasaṭena[^301-8] pañcamaṃ ukkācitaāmisa-garuvisama-adhammikā adhammādī ti (v.). Hita-accherakaṃ anutappa-thūpārahā atho pi dve buddhā asanī tayo kimpurisavijāyanam atha sannivāsasaṃsārena cā ti (vi.). Gihī ca kāma-upadhi-āsavasāmisañ ca ariyena kāyapītisātasamādhinivatti ca (vii.). Nidānañ ca hetusaṅkhārapaccayarūpaṃ vedayitaṃ saññaṃ viññāṇam yañ ca saṅkhataṃ vimuttipaggaho nāmaṃ (viii.).


<!-- PTS Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 302]*

Vijjā bhavesu diṭṭhi ahiri hiri dovacassam atha dhātuyo āpatti-vuṭṭhāna-kusalatā[^302-1] (ix.). Bālā ca kappiyāpatti adhammavinayena ca kukkuccakappiyāpatti adhammavinayena ca (x.). Puggalo subhanimittañ[^302-2] ca ceto bālena pañcamaṃ paññā asokapubbakārī[^302-3] ca vitthogo[^302-4] duttappa-paccayañ ca[^302-5] vuttagarukā lahukā[^302-6] duṭṭhullena cā ti (xi.). Āyācani cattāro khatehi[^302-7] ca durapari[^302-8]-sacittako vā vinaye[^302-9] cāgaṃ pariccāgaṃ bhogā sambhogā saṃvibhāgā saṅgāhamanuggāham atho pi anukampena cā ti (xii.-xiii.). Santhārā paṭisanthārā esanā pariyesanā pariyeṭṭhiyo pūjā ātitheyya-iddhi-vuḍḍhi-ratana-sannicayā[^302-10] (xiv.). Samāpatti ajjavañ ca khanti sākalyaṃ avihiṃsā dve indriyapaṭisaṅkhānasati[^302-11] samatho[^302-12] vipatti-sampadā-visuddhidiṭṭhi-asantuṭṭha-muṭṭhasaccena ca paññāsako (xv.). Dve dhammā sekho tañ ca kaveyyaṃ[^302-13] kusalānavajjañ[^302-14] ca sukhudrayañ ca vivekaṃ[^302-15] vyāpajjhasataṃ dukkhena ca tayo ca. Sammukhā dve pavāraṇā tajjaniyaṃ niyassañ ca pabbajaniyañ ca sārāṇaṃ ukkhepo parivāso ca mūlamānattaabbhānaṃ (xvi.-xvii.). Dutiyo nipāto. III. Bāla-lakkhaṇacittā[^302-16] accayaṃ[^302-17] ayonisena ca akusalasāvajja-savyāpajjha-duccarita-malena ca[^302-18] (1-10). Nātako sārāṇīyo nirāso[^302-19] cakkavatti Pacetaṇo apaṇṇakataṃ[^302-20] [attā] devā pāpaṇikā[^302-21] apara duve[^302-22] (11-20).


<!-- PTS Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 303]*

Kāyasakkhī gilāno saṅkhāro bahukāro arūko[^303-1] āsevitabbo jegucchi-pupphabhāṇī andho avakujjena ca[^303-2] (21-30). Sabrahmak'; Ānanda-Sāriputta-nidānam Āḷavakena ca[^303-3] devadūtā dve rājā sukhumālādhipatiyena ca vagga[^303-4] (31-40). Sammukhiṭṭhānaparesa-pavattanī paṇḍito sīlavā saṅkhataṃ pabbatātappa[^303-5]-mahācorena te dasa [paññāsako] (41-50). Dve janā[^303-6] brāhmaṇa-paribbājakā nibbānamahāsālena ca Vacchagottena[^303-7] ca Tikaṇṇo Jānussoṇi-Saṅgāravena[^303-8] ca[^303-9] (51-60). Titthaṃ bhayañ ca Venāgo Sarabho Kesaputtiyā[^303-10] Sāḷho ca kathā-vatthuṃ aññatitthiyā[^303-11] akusulamūla-uposathaṅgena te dasa[^303-12] (61-70). Channo[^303-13] ājīvako Sakko nigaṇṭhasamādapetabbena ca bhava-cetanā-patthanā-upaṭṭhāna-gandha-abhibhūna saha <Ānandavaggo (71-80).> Samaṇā[^303-14] ca yaṃ sukhettaṃ[^303-15] Vajjiputtaṃ sekhena pañca masāyo[^303-16] ca sādhikā vuttā[^303-17] dve sikkhā[^303-18] atha Paṅkadhāyena ca[^303-19] (81-90). Accāyikañ[^303-20] ca pavivekaṃ aggavatiparisā[^303-21] ca tayo ājānīyo vatthaṃ[^303-22] atha potthakaṃ loṇaphalena[^303-23] paṃsudhovakasuvaṇṇakārena ca paṇṇāsako[^303-24] (91-100). Pubbe pariyesanā assādo ruṇṇo[^303-25] tiṇṇaṃ atitti[^303-26] dve kūṭā dve nidānāni[^303-27] apare duve[^303-28] (101-110).


<!-- PTS Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 304]*

Apāyikā[^304-1] dullabho appameyyo ānañcāyatanena[^304-2] vipattiyo[^304-3] apaṇṇako kammantaṃ[^304-4] dve soceyyā moneyyena ca vaggo[^304-5] (111-120)[^304-6]. Kusināra-bhaṇḍana-Gotamakā Bharaṇḍu-Hatthakena ca kaṭuviyaṃ[^304-7] dve Anuruddhā paṭichanna-pāsāṇalekhena te dasa[^304-8] (121-130). Yodhā[^304-9] parisā mitto uppādakesakambalasampadā vuddhi tayo ca assakhaḷuṅka[^304-10] tayo ca moranivāpena vaggo[^304-11] (131-140). Akusalā sāvajjā visama-asucinā saha khato ca honti cattāri vandana-sukha-pubbaṇhena vaggo[^304-12] (141-150). Tikanipāto samatto.[^304-13]
